Status: RECORD

# The elemental-reaction census: what the seat records say fired, and how

A keyword census, run 2026-09-05 by `tools/reaction_census.py` over 116 blind-seat record files under `review/qa/` (every `klee-round-*`, `kokomi-round-*`, `furina-reframe-round-*` and `control-ironclad-*` directory), for the sweep packet's §4 item 2 (`review/ruled/elements-reaction-sweep-2026-09-05.md`, RULED R263; it was `git show 95b2882a:review/active/elements-reaction-sweep-2026-09-05.md` and not yet on `main` when this ran). It found 1473 mentions of the eight implemented reactions (Dendro's Burning is listed and reads zero -- §3 of that packet already says why). Every number below is produced by re-running the script; nothing here is hand-counted or hand-graded.

**This is a keyword count, not a reading.** A "mention" is one regex match of a reaction's name anywhere in a record's prose, including inside a quoted card or glossary text -- so a reaction named in a rule box counts exactly the same as one a fight says fired. "Trigger" and "decision vs. found-afterwards" are both literal, priority-ordered keyword matches over the sentence a mention falls in, not a judgement about what actually happened at the table. Where a sentence carries neither a decision-word nor a found-word, it is counted as **unmarked** rather than forced into either bucket -- and that bucket turns out to be the largest one, which is itself a finding.

## 6. Limits

This is regex over prose, not a reading of any fight. A reaction named in a rule quote or a keyword-box excerpt counts the same as one a record says fired. Four proper-noun collisions are excluded by name because they would otherwise swamp an unrelated reaction (the relic `Frozen Egg`, the relic `Burning Blood`, the card `Burning Pact`, Bennett's power `Passion Overload`); nothing else is hand-filtered, so `Freminet -- Shattering Pressure` and the base-game card `Shatter` both sit inside the Frozen row on purpose. "Sentence" is cut out of markdown prose by terminal punctuation or a paragraph break, not a real parser, so an occasional row runs on past what a human would call one sentence. Trigger and reading are both literal keyword buckets, checked in a fixed priority order (see the script's `TRIGGER_CHECKS`, `DECISION_RE`, `FOUND_RE`); "unmarked" is not a reading of "no decision happened", only of "neither signal phrase is here". The companion-acquisition count in §5 is a line count, not a reading, and does not name which companion.

No record's Identity section in this run prints a `Build` field, so that column is left out of the table below rather than shipped empty; the script still looks for one (`BUILD_RE`) and will fill it in the day a record does.
